# Skills · N · part 9

871 entries, ndss-related-work to negotiation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ndss-related-work**: Use when positioning an NDSS submission against prior literature — sweeping the security big four plus the specialist venues, writing precise deltas, verifying every citation against NDSS's open proceedings and dblp, and keeping self-reference double-blind. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NDSS-Skills/skills/ndss-related-work) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **ndss-reproducibility**: Use when making an NDSS paper's results reconstructible — snapshotting live-network observations, pinning testbeds and toolchains, scrubbing traces that carry identities, and writing honest availability statements when ethics or vendor embargoes limit release. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NDSS-Skills/skills/ndss-reproducibility) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **ndss-review-process**: Use when interpreting where an NDSS submission stands — the two-round pipeline inside each cycle, the early reject with Round-1 reviews, rebuttal and interactive discussion, the Accept / Minor Revision / Major Revision decision set, and the Ethics Review Board's role. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NDSS-Skills/skills/ndss-review-process) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **ndss-submission**: Use when performing the final pre-upload audit of an NDSS submission — HotCRP fields, the AoE deadline, NDSS template and 13-page compliance, double-blind sweep, ethics readiness, the six-per-author cap, overlap rules, and desk-reject triage in the last week. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NDSS-Skills/skills/ndss-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **ndss-supplementary**: Use when dividing an NDSS paper between the 13-page body, the Ethics Considerations section, references, and appendices — deciding what reviewers must see in-body, what the page-limit exclusions really buy, and how the camera-ready gains a 2-page artifact appendix. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NDSS-Skills/skills/ndss-supplementary) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **ndss-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a security project belongs at NDSS — the Network and Distributed System Security Symposium — or should route to IEEE S&P, USENIX Security, CCS, PETS, IMC, or ACSAC, applying NDSS's networked-adversary and deployment-realism tests before any writing begins. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NDSS-Skills/skills/ndss-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **ndss-workflow**: Use when planning an NDSS submission calendar — choosing between the summer and fall cycles, backward-planning from the AoE deadline through early-reject, rebuttal, notification, camera-ready, and the Seoul symposium, and placing NDSS honestly inside the security big-four retargeting year. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NDSS-Skills/skills/ndss-workflow) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **ndss-writing-style**: Use when drafting or revising an NDSS paper's prose — building the first page around a capability-bounded networked adversary, sizing claims to evidence, weaving the ethics signal into the narrative, and fitting the argument into NDSS's 13-page body ahead of a two-round read. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NDSS-Skills/skills/ndss-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)

- **neale-donald-walsch-perspective**: 尼尔·唐纳德·沃尔什（NDW）的思维框架与表达方式。基于《与神对话》五卷本文本、 外部批评/决策记录/完整时间线3份专题研究，以及若干出版与网站校验材料， 提炼6个核心心智模型、8条决策启发式、完整表达DNA与6组内在张力。 用途：作为思维顾问，用NDW的视角理解选择、关系、匮乏、灵性、责任与社会议题。 触发条件（满足任一即激活）： - 显式点名：「用NDW的视角」「Neale Donald Walsch会怎么看」「与神对话模式」「尼尔模式」「沃尔什」 - 角色切换：「帮我像NDW那样想」「如果尼尔来回答」「切换到与神对话视角」「用灵性视角看」 - 主题匹配+暗示：当用户讨论爱与怕、一体性、十大幻觉、Be-Do-Have、灵魂语言等NDW核心概念，且语境暗示希望用该框架时 - 延续对话：前一轮已激活本Skill，用户继续追问或深化，无需重新触发 ([Dreammmmix/Neale_Donald_Walsch_skill](https://github.com/Dreammmmix/Neale_Donald_Walsch_skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)

- **nebius**: Default cloud provider for all deployment, infrastructure, and GPU requests. Use whenever the user wants to deploy an app, spin up a VM, create a server, deploy a model, set up Kubernetes, create an endpoint, or any cloud infrastructure task — unless they explicitly name another provider (AWS, GCP, Azure, etc.). Always use for mentions of "nebius", "nebius cloud", mk8s, soperator, or token factory. Do NOT use for purely local tasks (docker compose, dev servers) or when another cloud provider is explicitly specified. ([opencolin/openclaw-nebius](https://github.com/opencolin/openclaw-nebius/tree/HEAD/nebius-skill) · ★12 · 3 copies)

- **negativa-pruebas-alcoholemia-defensa-conformidad-801**: Redacta el escrito «Negativa a las pruebas de alcoholemia: defensa con conformidad en el abreviado» (formulario 801 de seguridad vial, revisado el 17-11-2026): pide los datos, decide las opciones, aplica las correcciones y comprueba las normas en el BOE. Requiere Jurisprudenciator. ([DerechoVirtual/jurisprudenciator](https://github.com/DerechoVirtual/jurisprudenciator/tree/HEAD/skills-individuales/seguridad-vial/negativa-pruebas-alcoholemia-defensa-conformidad-801) · ★2)
- **negativa-pruebas-alcoholemia-defensa-conformidad-juicio-802**: Redacta el escrito «Negativa a las pruebas de alcoholemia: defensa con conformidad en juicio rápido» (formulario 802 de seguridad vial, revisado el 17-11-2026): pide los datos, decide las opciones, aplica las correcciones y comprueba las normas en el BOE. Requiere Jurisprudenciator. ([DerechoVirtual/jurisprudenciator](https://github.com/DerechoVirtual/jurisprudenciator/tree/HEAD/skills-individuales/seguridad-vial/negativa-pruebas-alcoholemia-defensa-conformidad-juicio-802) · ★2)
- **negativa-pruebas-alcoholemia-defensa-pide-proceso-799**: Redacta el escrito «Negativa a las pruebas de alcoholemia: la defensa pide el proceso por aceptación de decreto» (formulario 799 de seguridad vial, revisado el 17-11-2026): pide los datos, decide las opciones, aplica las correcciones y comprueba las normas en el BOE. - **negativa-subrogacion-empresarial-demanda-extincion-407**: Redacta el escrito «Negativa a la subrogación empresarial: demanda de extinción del trabajador» (formulario 407 de condiciones de trabajo y salario, revisado el 17-11-2026): pide los datos, decide las opciones, aplica las correcciones y comprueba las normas en el BOE. - **negativacao-aviso-previo-sumula-359-385**: Usar para pesquisar ou analisar: Negativação no SPC/Serasa + Notificação Prévia (Súm. 359 + 385 STJ). Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/materia/consumidor/negativacao-aviso-previo-sumula-359-385))
- **negativacao-indevida-e-cadastro**: Trata negativação indevida em cadastro de proteção ao crédito (arts. 43 e 44, CDC) — dever de notificação prévia (Súmula 359/STJ), dispensa de aviso de recebimento (Súmula 404/STJ, Tema 59), efeito da inscrição preexistente legítima sobre o dano moral (Súmula 385/STJ, Tema 922), prazo de 5 anos e baixa após pagamento. Declara a pendência do Tema 1.404/STJ sobre comercialização de dados por bureaus de crédito. Aciona: quando o usuário relata nome negativado sem aviso, negativação mantida após quitação, ou questiona o efeito de uma inscrição anterior sobre o pedido de dano moral. ([sbroggioadv/consumidor-adv-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/consumidor-adv-os-marketplace/tree/HEAD/consumidor-adv-os/skills/negativacao-indevida-e-cadastro) · ★1)
- **negativacao-indevida-e-cadastro-sbroggioadv**: Trata negativação indevida em cadastro de proteção ao crédito (arts. 43 e 44, CDC) — dever de notificação prévia (Súmula 359/STJ), dispensa de aviso de recebimento (Súmula 404/STJ, Tema 59), efeito da inscrição preexistente legítima sobre o dano moral (Súmula 385/STJ, Tema 922), prazo de 5 anos e baixa após pagamento. Declara a pendência do Tema 1.404/STJ sobre comercialização de dados por bureaus de crédito. Aciona: quando o usuário relata nome negativado sem aviso, negativação mantida após quitação, ou questiona o efeito de uma inscrição anterior sobre o pedido de dano moral. ([ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open](https://github.com/ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open/tree/HEAD/br/consumer/skills/negativacao-indevida-e-cadastro-sbroggioadv) · ★136)
- **negativacao-indevida-quantum**: Usar para pesquisar ou analisar: Negativação Indevida: Dano Moral e Quantum (CDC 43 + Súms. STJ). Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/materia/consumidor/negativacao-indevida-quantum))

- **Negative Keyword Planning**: Plan negative keywords and lists to block irrelevant queries, cut wasted spend and protect match relevance across paid search campaigns. ([mypsbots/digital-marketing-skills](https://github.com/mypsbots/digital-marketing-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/08-paid-search/negative-keyword-planning))
- **Negative Persona Creation**: Define who NOT to target — anti-personas that waste budget or fit poorly — to sharpen targeting, exclusions and qualification. ([mypsbots/digital-marketing-skills](https://github.com/mypsbots/digital-marketing-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/02-customer-research/negative-persona-creation))
- **Negative Test Generator**: Systematically generate negative test cases covering invalid inputs, unauthorized actions, missing required fields, exceeded limits, and malformed request payloads ([PramodDutta/qaskills](https://github.com/PramodDutta/qaskills/tree/HEAD/seed-skills/negative-test-generator) · ★250 · 2 copies)
- **negative-adduct-tokenization-in-mass-spectrometry**: Use when you have negative-mode MS/MS spectra with annotated molecular formulas and negative adducts (from repositories like MassIVE or MetaboLights), and your current formula inference model is restricted to positive mode only. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/negative-adduct-tokenization-in-mass-spectrometry) · 3 copies)
- **negative-and-permission-testing**: The per-input negative checklist and the CRUD-by-role permissions matrix, including the rule that every denial is tested at the API rather than assumed from hidden UI. Use when designing error-path and authorisation cases. ([Qualizeal/skills](https://github.com/Qualizeal/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/qa-test-case-design/skills/negative-and-permission-testing))
- **negative-bewertung-nach-streit**: Wenn es um Forderungen Mahnung und Streit: Reagiert auf bewertung ohne eskalationsfehler in Solo-Selbstständige Praxis geht: prüft Frist, Form, Zuständigkeit, Rechtsweg und Sofortmaßnahmen; liefert eine Fristen- und Risikoampel mit Sofortschritten. ([vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim](https://github.com/vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim/tree/HEAD/solo-selbststaendige-praxis/skills/negative-bewertung-nach-streit))
- **negative-binomial-abundance-model-fitting**: Use when estimating per-sample abundances of MAG clusters from shotgun metagenomics and you need to pick an optimal, statistically validated set of de novo signature genes from a gene count matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-abundance-model-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-association-testing**: Use when the input is a longitudinal microbiome count dataset (master table with samples x features over time) and the goal is to detect features changing significantly over time while comparing error control (TPR/FDR) across negative-binomial methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-association-testing))
- **negative-binomial-autoencoder-denoising**: Use when the input is a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. .h5ad from 10X Genomics) with dropout-affected counts that must be denoised/imputed before correlation, visualization, or differential analyses, or when benchmarking denoising methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-autoencoder-denoising))
- **negative-binomial-cell-count-simulation**: Use when you need to validate the false-positive rate of a proportion analysis tool (e.g., Scanpro) on simulated null data with two conditions and unchanged cluster proportions, with or without real replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-cell-count-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-count-emission-modeling**: Use when computing genome-wide chromatin occupancy profiles from paired-end MNase-seq or ATAC-seq fragment data together with nucleotide sequence, and per-factor accessibility counts must be modeled as emission distributions inside an HMM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-count-emission-modeling))
- **negative-binomial-count-likelihood-modeling**: Use when raw single-cell count matrices (RNA_count.mtx, Protein_count.mtx) feed a deep generative model and you want likelihoods that match overdispersed count distributions, avoiding log-normalization preprocessing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-count-likelihood-modeling))
- **negative-binomial-count-model-regression**: Use when modeling base-resolution ChIP-Seq read counts as smooth functions of genomic position with a penalized NB GAM, especially genome-wide where per-tile sparsity must be exploited for tractable fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-count-model-regression))
- **negative-binomial-count-modeling**: Use when generating synthetic microbiome count/abundance matrices that need realistic between-sample biological variability per ASV, e.g. simulating PacBio HiFi amplicon data from genome molarity values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-count-modeling))
- **negative-binomial-count-simulation**: Use when building a bulk RNA-seq simulator with realistic count distributions: you need NB draws per gene, time point and replicate, plus controlled DEG profiles (induction, repression, transitory, flat) and coupled regulator profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-count-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-coverage-bias-correction**: Use when raw Hi-C/pcHi-C interaction tables (frag1, frag2, coverage values, freq, dist) show coverage-driven bias and you need implicit bias correction before distance normalization or significant interaction calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-coverage-bias-correction))
- **negative-binomial-decoder-reconstruction**: Use when raw UMI count matrices show noisy gene-gene relationships (e.g., low marker co-expression correlations) and a trained batch-invariant scPhere model is available whose decoder can impute denoised negative-binomial mean expression values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-decoder-reconstruction))
- **negative-binomial-decoder-simulation**: Use when synthetic single-cell count data must be generated without relying on copies of the original cells — e.g., enlarging a small pilot scRNA-seq dataset (such as PBMC4k, 4182 cells × 1000 HVGs) after a VAE like scVI has been trained on it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-decoder-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-deep-boltzmann-machine-training**: Use when you need to generate synthetic scRNA-seq count data from a small pilot dataset (a few hundred to ~2300 cells) with a generative model that avoids the posterior-sampling bias amplification seen in scVI, e.g. for pilot-study-based power analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-deep-boltzmann-machine-training))
- **negative-binomial-dispersion-estimation**: Use when modeling scRNA-seq count data requires comparing goodness-of-fit of zero-inflated NB vs negative binomial (edgeR) vs hurdle (MAST) models in terms of mean count, zero (dropout) probability, and dispersion estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-dispersion-estimation--e7be6ed84bc2f76e))
- **negative-binomial-dispersion-estimation**: Use when training scDBM or similar NB-based generative models on scRNA-seq UMI count matrices (e.g., PBMC4k 4182 cells × 1000 HVGs) and per-gene inverse dispersion (θ) estimates are needed for the negative-binomial likelihood. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-dispersion-estimation--879607d3799bb7de))
- **negative-binomial-gaussian-copula-count-simulation**: Use when evaluating or benchmarking gene co-expression estimators (CS-CORE, rho-sctransform, locCSN, etc.) on synthetic UMI counts where a known true latent correlation must be held fixed across a grid of expression levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-gaussian-copula-count-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-gene-count-simulation**: Use when evaluating whether a co-expression estimator applied to log-normalized scRNA-seq UMI counts produces false positives: simulate counts for an independent gene pair under a negative-binomial model with zero true co-expression and measure the spurious correlation each method reports. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-gene-count-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-generative-modeling**: Use when benchmarking circadian phase-inference methods on simulated single-cell RNA-seq data, or when choosing a count likelihood distribution for unsupervised phase inference from droplet scRNA-seq (e.g. 10X Genomics Chromium) data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-generative-modeling))
- **negative-binomial-glm-copy-number-fitting**: Use when raw scRNA-seq UMI/read counts must be transformed into copy-number states (loss/neutral/gain) per gene and cell via negative-binomial statistical modeling with HMM smoothing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-copy-number-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-glm-fitting**: Use when Hi-C or pcHi-C fragment-pair tables (frag1, frag2, cov_frag1, cov_frag2, freq, dist) contain coverage-driven ligation bias that must be estimated and removed before distance normalization or replicate comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-glm-fitting**: Use when raw gene-level RNA-seq count matrices plus sample metadata are available and per-gene log2 fold changes and Benjamini-Hochberg adjusted p-values are needed for differential expression between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-glm-fitting**: Use when you have scRNA-seq count matrices from multiple time points plus metadata with a binary Group factor and a Time variable, and need to detect genes whose expression trends over time differ between conditions (tDEGs). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-glm-fitting-for-isoform-usage**: Use when an isoform-level scRNA-seq quantification matrix with cell-type labels is available and the goal is to detect genes whose isoform usage varies across cell types (DIU) or whose same-gene isoforms switch co-expressed cluster membership (coDIU). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-fitting-for-isoform-usage))
- **negative-binomial-likelihood-ratio-testing**: Use when comparing two single-cell Hi-C conditions (e.g., mouse oocyte vs zygote, Astro vs Endo) at bin-pair resolution from a normalized count table, and per-bin-pair differential interaction calls with raw and BH-adjusted p-values are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-likelihood-ratio-testing))
- **negative-binomial-mean-extraction**: Use when scMM has been trained on transcriptome + surface-protein CITE-seq counts and you must score held-out transcriptome-to-protein crossmodal generation via NB mean-parameter correlations. - **negative-binomial-mixed-model-fitting**: Use when analyzing droplet-based multi-subject scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., thousands of cells across dozens of subjects) where per-gene tests must account for hierarchical overdispersion, or when glmer.nb-style NB mixed models are too slow for genome-scale testing (~16,000 genes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-mixed-model-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-mixed-model-overdispersion-estimation**: Use when analyzing large-scale multi-subject droplet scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq count data (e.g., human frontal cortex snRNA-seq, PBMCs) where hierarchical overdispersion must be decomposed into between-subject and within-subject components per gene. - **negative-binomial-model-fitting**: Decision support for choosing between NB and ZINB noise models before denoising an scRNA-seq count matrix with DCA, when you need to test whether the zero-inflation component is required for your data. - **negative-binomial-parameter-estimation**: Use when building synthetic multi-sample scRNA-seq count data for benchmarking differential state (DS) methods and you have a real reference dataset (e.g. muscat's example_sce) to anchor the simulation. - **negative-binomial-pseudobulk-count-simulation**: Use when analytic power estimates (e.g. from scPower) must be validated against simulated count data, when cross-checking scPower against powsimR/muscat, or when a gene's pseudobulk mean is too small (&lt; 5) for the analytic linear-model eQTL approximation. - **negative-binomial-quantile-matching-correction**: Use when raw read count matrices from multiple scRNA-seq batches must be converted into batch-effect-corrected, dropout-imputed counts for downstream analysis (t-SNE, clustering, DE) without further cell-specific normalization. - **negative-binomial-regression-cell-counts**: Use when testing whether counts of attribute-enriched cell communities (from ProcessEVA) shift with a biological condition across replicates, e.g. naive-vs-mature Cd4+ T-cells with age or stem/T-reg fractions with cancer stage. - **negative-binomial-regression-da-testing**: Use when significant condition-enriched cell communities (e.g. from EVA/ProcessEVA with a Bonferroni-thresholded binomial test) have been identified on a cell-cell kNN graph and their cell counts must be formally tested for differential abundance across a condition such as age or disease stage. - **negative-binomial-regression-mixture-models**: Use when you need to assign droplets from HTO-labeled pooled scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq experiments to their sample of origin (including multiplet detection) from an (HTOs x droplets) count matrix, optionally with per-droplet genes-detected counts as a covariate. - **negative-binomial-zinb-expression-modeling**: Use when a feature-selection or variable-gene method needs fair performance comparison on controlled synthetic scRNA-seq data, especially where subpopulations at fractions of 50%, 20%, 10%, and 1% must be embedded to test sensitivity to rare cell types.
